ParlaMint-ES-GA_2015-05-26-DSPG110.u238	To formulate the non-law proposition has the word Mr. Juan Fajardo Recouso.
ParlaMint-ES-GA_2015-05-26-DSPG110.u239	Good afternoon again in this lovely afternoon session. We have taken an initiative that is not because it is hidden in the data is less important, and that is why we want to bring it to Parliament's plenary session. There are some issues that are not everyday, that don't come out in the major press headlines, but that are realities they face, not tens of thousands of workers every day in this country. And in this country there's already been a new term, which is the workaholic zombie, which is that worker who's going to work every day because he has to go, because he has to meet, because he has to lock, because he has to produce, but when he comes to the end of the month he doesn't charge his payroll. These workers work in many companies, even in companies that work in the administration. In Galicia today we can see how over 600 Telare workers go every day to their shop, sell clothes, enter that money, but it turns out that in their payrolls there is not a single weight when it comes to the end of the month. Also the workers of Foelca, in the region of Barbanza – who received tens of millions of the public archives that company – have been unpaid for months, and in some case more than nine months. Workers elsewhere in the state, Everest, Lanas Stop, from the media like Editorial La Capital, who don't receive their payroll every month. That's a reality that, of course, if you don't suffer it, you don't know what it means. Because, in addition to the suffering of not having wages, you have the added suffering of going to perform a job for which you have no remuneration. And that was an isolated element long ago, now it's even a method of extortion. The People's Party has managed to introduce into labor relations a new mechanism, a new variable, which is the variable of fear: the variable of fear to mobilize you because there's nothing out there, the variable of fear to mobilize you because it's possible that this penalizes you in future jobs and the variable of going to trial, because everything is recorded and then the next employer can take into account that at some point you claimed what a right is. And so those issues are tremendously complex. And behind the reduction data of ERE extinction there are issues like these, entrepreneurs who even receive criteria from business groups like that, if they're planning to fire, it's best at first to stop paying payrolls. Because a worker who doesn't receive his payroll often doesn't hold up and therefore in many cases abandon his job; which means that they won't be able to pay those payrolls, they won't be able to collect compensation and they won't be able to charge unemployment, in addition, because there's no legal unemployment situation. To avoid these issues we should do something, because the only way these workers have is to claim before the social court, and as soon as three months of payrolls arrive; to demand for them to be paid or claimed to be cleared of the contract and, therefore, to be without their job. Given that we have to wait three months and the social waiting list can be six, seven or nine months, we are with workers who are absolutely unprotected. It's even more: they're unprotected from the administration, because the Labour Counsellor himself knew that the labour authority, who can issue a wage-free penalty, doesn't have to pay those wages. I mean, as a worker, I'm in my company, they don't pay the salary, I call the Labour Inspectorate, an act of infringement is lifted, and the act of infringement means a fine to the employer but in no case requires him to pay the salary immediately. So that whole set of conditions is making the labour market in this country now have a new element, which is that situation of workers who have to produce without that production being compensated for by wages. In that sense, we raise a question that is quite simple: that a mechanism should be set up from the Board so that, in the process of employers' defaulting demonstrated – we believe that demonstrated in a resolution of the Labour Court, which is much more agile than a judicial resolution, although, if they have it, it is worth it – if you articulate some mechanism so that the Board itself can anticipate those wages, it can compensate those wages, since it has far more effective and agile formulas to charge the company that the workers themselves have. And that's our proposal. It is clear that this is a situation that we all know except the People's Party, because the changes in the contributions of opposition groups are improving our proposal, that what we want to put on the table is a very serious problem that is never taken into account, the most serious problem of a new working relationship mechanism and a new pressure mechanism. Why is it that when a job reform is done it is always to cut down rights and not to recognize, for example, the employee employed the obligation to receive his salary in time and form and agile management mechanisms to recover that salary? Why can't that be? Because the People's Party is an accomplice and an expert of this new reality, the reality that it is supposed to use these mechanisms in the end does not pay any wages. So we're talking about a new conception of working relationships, in a place where you've already paid the salary doesn't even mean you have the ability to get loosely by the end of the month. And I put a data on the table. Perhaps any of the parliamentarians who are here today, if a month does not receive Parliament's salary, nothing would happen. I'm sure some would protest at minute two not to receive their salary, but it wouldn't happen, because I would have the ability to, with previous wages, to resist. But the situation out there, of the labour market, of the reality of the workers who go to their post every day, is not that. Because one in three workers in this country, one in three, receive less than 645 euros gross a month. So imagine, when a worker receives just 600 euros each month for his work, which is not only a delay but a single default, he assumes a lot of inconvenience in many cases to lose his home, to cuts in the light, to supply cuts, and even to an aberration that is given in this country, which is the following: that is, before default to your municipality in a tax, at a fee, not because of your responsibility but that a company has not paid you and you cannot justify a resolution, you automatically stay out of any kind of contribution. And so there are workers in this situation who have lost, for example, their children's scholarships because they're not aware of the public farm, because they couldn't afford to pay even though they're working, because they don't get that salary. And it's the worker's responsibility, not the employer who's not paying that salary. So we, our proposal – and we finish – is very simple: that this mechanism is an articulate that allows, by means of the formula that is specified in a timely manner by the technicians of the Union, mechanisms of immediate collection of these wages so that workers do not have situations like those that live every day. And, therefore, I end by sending solidarity from our group – and we will bring initiatives here in that sense – to the workers of the Tellares, who have been unpaid for months and have not received any kind of help from the Administration, who should look after their situation. And on the other hand, by trade unions and leftist political organizations, which we believe these kinds of injustices cannot be accepted. Nothing else and thank you very much.

The advantages of learning oral expression techniques would be a lot for the student, thinking both of his present and of his future. In the short term, prayer would help them to do better at school, to understand, to interpret, to communicate, to express and to relate. And in the future, it must be borne in mind that a student trained in communication, argumentation and expressiveness has more possibilities in a globalized and competitive world, possibilities to stand out, to integrate into a team, to have the ability to work as a team, to look for work, or even to create and manage his own company. This vision of linguistic communication linked to social practices is an image of the person as a communicative agent, which, in addition to receiving messages, also has to produce them, so competition in linguistic communication represents a path of knowledge and contact with social and cultural diversity. The domain of oral expression is one of the transversal goals specific to secondary education, and it must work on all subjects. In addition, the necessary measures must be developed so that their ability to express themselves properly in public is at work from the curricular and methodological point of view. Discursive capacity itself is clearly linked to the development of critical knowledge and social participation, in addition to the possibility of peacefully resolving conflicts and strengthening attitudes of initiative, creativity, teamwork and self-confidence. In Galicia there are already successful experiences that show the interest among the younger in this type of dialectic-related activities, oratory-related activities, oral expression and debate. We therefore consider that encouraging the teaching oratory allows students to learn to speak in public through a preparation based on structured and critical acquisition of information. And also, in academic activity, oratory must be a cross-sectional competition, but you can enable measures that help its implementation in teaching. Oratory can be worked out from four basic areas: information, argumentation, communication and persuasion. All this seems to us to contribute to improving the educational ability of students, as well as adequately preparing them for the challenges that society and the world of work will present to them. For all this, we present for your approval the following proposal: "The Galician Parliament urges the Galician Union to take the necessary measures to deepen the implementation of educational programmes, methodologies and even materials that promote improvement in the skills in oral expression through discursive practices, oratory and argumentation that result in a greater expressive capacity of the Greek student of compulsory secondary education and of Bachelorship." ParlaMint-ES-GA_2015-05-26-DSPG110.u287	All right, let's go. Good afternoon, everyone. Well, the reality is that rhetorical discipline was born in ancient Greece around the year 485 before Christ, when tyrants Iron and Hero expropriated the lands of the peasants to hand them over to the members of their personal army. They were, therefore, swindled people and people suffocated by the tyrants who suffered the power and oppression of their leaders. With the arrival of Greek democracy, and with the overthrow of tyrants, many of these expropriated peasants claimed the return of their lands through prices, and there the use of rhetoric was a fundamental element for, precisely, bringing their causes forward. Therefore, rhetoric began as a fundamental weapon of legal language and then extended, through the academies, to the rest of the disciplines. The Rector was the master of rhetoric and therefore basically cultivated dialectics, controversy, critical capacity, debate, discussion... those are the tools of rhetoric and oratory. All these are the tools that fall down on the ground through the approval of LOMCE where it is established, is driven, an absolutely uniform, acrytic learning and, of course, impoverishing what it is, of course, what we consider a progressive learning towards a more advanced society. Galicia has several natural parks, but none of them are in Lugo province, despite the fact that Coruel is the largest natural space in Galicia, the one with the greatest diversity and geodiversity and what counts with a unique cultural and landscape heritage. And I warn you that The Courel is being condemned to have no future, to have no future!, despite being one of the greatest potential beauty and singularity of the Iberian Peninsula, and that it has various protection figures but that, far from palliating its deterioration, they contribute to increasing it by restricting traditional actions and works that, on the contrary, should instead of being restricted, be protected to preserve the natural and cultural heritage. Thus, the recently approved Nature Network Director Plan divided The Courel into three areas according to the level of protection, considering at the highest level the three best gain villages and their territories – Teixeira, Noceda and Vila – by applying the maximum restrictions on all farms when Europe only asks for protection in a perfectly localizable microparcels, so these types of decisions aggravate the discontent of the neighbors with the current protection figures and the lack of rigour in their elaboration. However, for the natural park declaration it is necessary to prepare documentation, with a natural resource management plan (PORN), also a master plan for use and management, which must count – these should indeed count – inevitably with the participation of neighbours, in addition to technicians, scientists, and administrations. With this, the identification and location of priority habitats and species would be linked to specific locations and locations, discarding absurd generic limitations such as those currently operating. Therefore, the natural park declaration would not only increase the generic restrictions of the Natura Network for neighbors, but would be more beneficial to concrete ones and adjust them to perfectly delimited territories containing small priority habitats. That's why it can't be claimed that there is a strong neighboring opposition – no, no... – to which The Courel is declared a natural park, when the area is already experiencing priority habitat restrictions in an extensive and unreasonably rational way, and made behind its inhabitants.
